   │         <script>\n
   │             var listAttApi;\n
   │             var posAttApi = 0;\n
   │             $(document).ready(function() {\n
   │                 $('.select2').select2({theme: "bootstrap"});\n
   │                 $('.select2tag').select2({theme: "bootstrap", tags: true});\n
   │                 setTimeout(() => {\n
   │                     listAttApi = document.querySelectorAll(`[api-get-text]`);\n
   │                     apiGetText();\n
   │                 }, 500);\n
   │             });\n
   │ \n
   │             // legacy pages: window.confirm based confirmation (the WP6A screens use the ConfirmSheet / DangerDialog)\n
   │             function ask(field, text) {\n
   │                 return confirm(text);\n
   │             }\n
   │ \n
   │             function apiGetText() {\n
   │                 var el = listAttApi[posAttApi];\n
   │                 if (el != undefined) {\n
   │                     $.get(el.getAttribute('api-get-text'), function(data) {\n
   │                         el.innerHTML = data;\n
   │                         posAttApi++;\n
   │                         if (posAttApi < listAttApi.length) {\n
   │                             apiGetText();\n
   │                         }\n
   │                     });\n
   │                 }\n
   │             }\n
   │ \n
   │             function setKolaps() {\n
   │                 var kolaps = getCookie('kolaps');\n
   │                 if (kolaps) {\n
   │                     setCookie('kolaps', false, 30);\n
   │                 } else {\n
   │                     setCookie('kolaps', true, 30);\n
   │                 }\n
   │                 return true;\n
   │             }\n
   │ \n
   │             function setCookie(name, value, days) {\n
   │                 var expires = "";\n

   │                         }\n

   │ \n
   │             function setKolaps() {\n
   │                 var kolaps = getCookie('kolaps');\n
   │                 if (kolaps) {\n
   │                     setCookie('kolaps', false, 30);\n
   │                 } else {\n
   │                     setCookie('kolaps', true, 30);\n
   │                 }\n
   │                 return true;\n
   │             }\n
   │ \n
   │             function setCookie(name, value, days) {\n
   │                 var expires = "";\n
   │                 if (days) {\n
   │                     var date = new Date();\n
   │                     date.setTime(date.getTime() + (days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));\n
   │                     expires = "; expires=" + date.toUTCString();\n
   │                 }\n
   │                 document.cookie = name + "=" + (value || "") + expires + "; path=/";\n
   │             }\n
   │ \n
   │             function getCookie(name) {\n
   │                 var nameEQ = name + "=";\n
   │                 var ca = document.cookie.split(';');\n
   │                 for (var i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {\n
   │                     var c = ca[i];\n
   │                     while (c.charAt(0) == ' ') c = c.substring(1, c.length);\n
   │                     if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) == 0) return c.substring(nameEQ.length, c.length);\n
   │                 }\n
   │                 return null;\n
   │             }\n

   │     window.nuxrCsrfToken = "[redacted]";\n
   │ </script>\n
   │ \n
   │ <script>\n
   │     (function () {\n
   │         var token = window.nuxrCsrfToken;\n
   │         if (!token) { return; }\n
   │         function sameOrigin(u) {\n
   │             try { return new URL(u, window.location.href).origin === window.location.origin; } catch (e) { return false; }\n
   │         }\n
   │         function addToken(form) {\n
   │             if (!form || String(form.getAttribute('method') || form.method || '').toLowerCase() !== 'post') { return; }\n
   │             var existing = form.querySelector('input[name="csrf_token"]');\n
   │             if (existing) { if (!existing.value) { existing.value = token; } return; }\n
   │             var input = document.createElement('input');\n
   │             input.type = 'hidden'; input.name = 'csrf_token'; input.value = token;\n
   │             form.appendChild(input);\n
   │         }\n
   │         function addAll() { for (var i = 0; i < document.forms.length; i++) { addToken(document.forms[i]); } }\n
   │         document.addEventListener('submit', function (e) { addToken(e.target); }, true);\n
   │         if (document.readyState === 'loading') { document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', addAll); } else { addAll(); }\n
   │         var nativeSubmit = HTMLFormElement.prototype.submit;\n
   │         HTMLFormElement.prototype.submit = function () { addToken(this); return nativeSubmit.apply(this, arguments); };\n
   │         var xOpen = XMLHttpRequest.prototype.open, xSend = XMLHttpRequest.prototype.send;\n
   │         XMLHttpRequest.prototype.open = function (m, u) { this._nuxrSame = sameOrigin(u); return xOpen.apply(this, arguments); };\n
   │         XMLHttpRequest.prototype.send = function () {\n
   │             if (this._nuxrSame) { try { this.setRequestHeader('X-CSRF-Token', token); } catch (e) { } }\n
   │             return xSend.apply(this, arguments);\n
   │         };\n
   │         if (window.fetch) {\n
   │             var nativeFetch = window.fetch;\n
   │             window.fetch = function (input, init) {\n
   │                 var url = (typeof input === 'string') ? input : (input && input.url);\n
   │                 if (sameOrigin(url)) {\n
   │                     init = init || {};\n
   │                     var h = new Headers(init.headers || (typeof input !== 'string' && input.headers) || {});\n
   │                     h.set('X-CSRF-Token', token);\n
   │                     init.headers = h;\n
   │                 }\n
   │                 return nativeFetch.call(this, input, init);\n
   │             };\n
   │         }\n
   │         // POST navigation for script-driven mutations (replaces window.location.href = url)\n
   │         window.nuxrPost = function (url) {\n
   │             var form = document.createElement('form');\n
   │             form.method = 'post';\n
   │             form.action = url;\n
   │             form.style.display = 'none';\n
   │             addToken(form);\n
   │             document.body.appendChild(form);\n
   │             nativeSubmit.call(form);\n
   │         };\n
   │         document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {\n
   │             var a = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('a[data-method="post"]') : null;\n
   │             if (!a || e.defaultPrevented) { return; }\n
   │             e.preventDefault();\n
   │             var form = document.createElement('form');\n
   │             form.method = 'post';\n
   │             form.action = a.href;\n
   │             if (a.target) { form.target = a.target; }\n
   │             form.style.display = 'none';\n
   │             addToken(form);\n
   │             document.body.appendChild(form);\n
   │             nativeSubmit.call(form);\n
